Does an internal signature move before the behavioural gap does?

  0.00          79.2%         61.1%     10.5%        1.9%   -5.309   107.7

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16       nan%        nan% 
   0.05-0.15      45      42.9%       50.0% 
   0.15-0.35      68      50.0%       70.0% 
       >0.35     367      95.5%       86.5% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

  0.00          75.5%         47.2%     70.3%        3.6%   -1.436    39.5

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16      21.4%       26.7% 
   0.05-0.15      45      44.0%       48.4% 
   0.15-0.35      68      36.7%       37.3% 
       >0.35     367      90.0%       89.6% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

  0.00          70.3%         51.1%     90.6%        3.4%   -0.199    17.5

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16      21.4%       25.0% 
   0.05-0.15      45      47.6%       46.5% 
   0.15-0.35      68      55.0%       31.1% 
       >0.35     367      97.0%       82.9% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

  0.00          63.4%         56.8%     96.3%        3.0%    0.108    11.6

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16      46.7%       25.0% 
   0.05-0.15      45      61.9%       47.7% 
   0.15-0.35      68      71.0%       29.9% 
       >0.35     367      97.8%       73.5% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

  0.00          60.8%         59.0%     98.2%        3.2%    0.210     9.6

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16      40.0%       25.0% 
   0.05-0.15      45      65.1%       46.7% 
   0.15-0.35      68      80.3%       31.8% 
       >0.35     367      99.2%       69.4% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

  0.00          60.5%         59.6%     94.7%        2.9%    0.001    13.3

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16      40.0%       26.7% 
   0.05-0.15      45      72.7%       46.7% 
   0.15-0.35      68      85.5%       29.2% 
       >0.35     367      99.4%       69.7% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16      50.0%       25.0% 
   0.05-0.15      45      71.4%       46.7% 
   0.15-0.35      68      86.4%       30.8% 
       >0.35     367      99.5%       68.7% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

  0.00          57.3%         62.4%     98.6%        3.2%    0.212     9.3

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16      31.2%       25.0% 
   0.05-0.15      45      65.9%       46.7% 
   0.15-0.35      68      89.4%       29.4% 
       >0.35     367      99.7%       65.3% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

  0.00          58.0%         61.8%     98.0%        3.2%    0.185     9.9

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16      37.5%       25.0% 
   0.05-0.15      45      62.8%       46.7% 
   0.15-0.35      68      85.1%       30.8% 
       >0.35     367      99.7%       65.8% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

  0.00          54.9%         64.6%     99.2%        3.1%    0.227     8.9

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16      50.0%       25.0% 
   0.05-0.15      45      68.9%       46.7% 
   0.15-0.35      68      92.5%       29.4% 
       >0.35     367     100.0%       62.0% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

  0.00          53.0%         66.3%     99.6%        3.1%    0.242     8.5

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16      43.8%       25.0% 
   0.05-0.15      45      73.3%       46.7% 
   0.15-0.35      68      94.0%       29.4% 
       >0.35     367      99.7%       59.5% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

  0.00          56.5%         63.0%     98.6%        3.0%    0.210     9.3

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16      37.5%       25.0% 
   0.05-0.15      45      75.6%       46.7% 
   0.15-0.35      68      90.8%       30.3% 
       >0.35     367     100.0%       63.9% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16      56.2%       25.0% 
   0.05-0.15      45      77.3%       46.7% 
   0.15-0.35      68      93.9%       29.9% 
       >0.35     367     100.0%       60.3% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

  0.00          54.9%         64.5%     99.6%        3.1%    0.255     8.4

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16      37.5%       25.0% 
   0.05-0.15      45      64.4%       46.7% 
   0.15-0.35      68      97.1%       29.4% 
       >0.35     367      99.7%       61.9% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

  0.00          53.2%         66.0%    100.0%        3.1%    0.266     8.1

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16      43.8%       25.0% 
   0.05-0.15      45      71.1%       46.7% 
   0.15-0.35      68      89.6%       29.4% 
       >0.35     367      99.7%       59.7% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

  0.00          53.9%         65.1%     95.1%        3.3%   -0.033    13.5

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16      53.3%       25.0% 
   0.05-0.15      45      70.5%       47.6% 
   0.15-0.35      68      92.1%       28.8% 
       >0.35     367     100.0%       60.8% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

  0.00          54.3%         65.0%     99.8%        3.1%    0.262     8.2

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16      43.8%       25.0% 
   0.05-0.15      45      68.9%       46.7% 
   0.15-0.35      68      91.0%       29.4% 
       >0.35     367     100.0%       61.2% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

  0.00          54.8%         64.8%     98.6%        3.2%    0.194     9.5

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16      33.3%       25.0% 
   0.05-0.15      45      60.0%       46.7% 
   0.15-0.35      68      92.3%       29.9% 
       >0.35     367     100.0%       61.8% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

  0.00          52.6%         66.7%     99.2%        3.1%    0.215     8.9

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16      43.8%       25.0% 
   0.05-0.15      45      75.0%       46.7% 
   0.15-0.35      68      98.5%       29.9% 
       >0.35     367     100.0%       58.8% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16      56.2%       25.0% 
   0.05-0.15      45      66.7%       46.7% 
   0.15-0.35      68      91.2%       29.9% 
       >0.35     367     100.0%       60.8% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

  0.00          50.5%         68.6%     99.6%        2.9%    0.224     8.6

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16      25.0%       25.0% 
   0.05-0.15      45      66.7%       46.7% 
   0.15-0.35      68      97.1%       29.4% 
       >0.35     367     100.0%       56.0% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

  0.00          48.3%         70.9%     99.4%        3.1%    0.198     8.9

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16      33.3%       25.0% 
   0.05-0.15      45      80.0%       46.7% 
   0.15-0.35      68      97.0%       29.9% 
       >0.35     367     100.0%       52.9% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

  0.00          46.3%         72.9%     98.8%        3.2%    0.149     9.6

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16      56.2%       25.0% 
   0.05-0.15      45      84.4%       46.7% 
   0.15-0.35      68     100.0%       30.3% 
       >0.35     367     100.0%       50.1% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

  0.00          48.4%         70.7%    100.0%        3.1%    0.231     8.3

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16      25.0%       25.0% 
   0.05-0.15      45      66.7%       46.7% 
   0.15-0.35      68      98.5%       29.4% 
       >0.35     367     100.0%       53.1% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

  0.00          48.0%         71.1%    100.0%        3.1%    0.229     8.3

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16      37.5%       25.0% 
   0.05-0.15      45      75.6%       46.7% 
   0.15-0.35      68     100.0%       29.4% 
       >0.35     367     100.0%       52.6% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

  0.00          47.6%         71.7%     98.8%        3.2%    0.152     9.6

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16      43.8%       25.0% 
   0.05-0.15      45      70.5%       47.7% 
   0.15-0.35      68      97.0%       29.4% 
       >0.35     367     100.0%       51.9% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16      31.2%       25.0% 
   0.05-0.15      45      77.8%       46.7% 
   0.15-0.35      68      95.5%       29.4% 
       >0.35     367     100.0%       51.9% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

  0.00          48.2%         71.2%     98.4%        3.2%    0.136     9.9

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16      42.9%       25.0% 
   0.05-0.15      45      74.4%       46.7% 
   0.15-0.35      68      98.5%       29.9% 
       >0.35     367     100.0%       52.8% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

  0.00          51.0%         68.0%     97.1%        3.0%    0.074    11.3

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16      43.8%       25.0% 
   0.05-0.15      45      70.5%       45.5% 
   0.15-0.35      68      89.2%       30.3% 
       >0.35     367      99.7%       56.7% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

  0.00          59.2%         61.8%     91.4%        3.2%   -0.205    16.7

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16      35.7%       25.0% 
   0.05-0.15      45      61.0%       46.5% 
   0.15-0.35      68      88.7%       32.3% 
       >0.35     367      99.1%       67.5% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

  0.00          51.3%         68.0%     98.8%        3.0%    0.178     9.5

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16      50.0%       26.7% 
   0.05-0.15      45      72.7%       46.7% 
   0.15-0.35      68      92.5%       29.9% 
       >0.35     367      99.7%       56.9% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

  0.00          50.8%         68.5%     99.2%        3.1%    0.201     9.0

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16      25.0%       25.0% 
   0.05-0.15      45      66.7%       46.7% 
   0.15-0.35      68      92.5%       29.4% 
       >0.35     367     100.0%       56.5% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

  0.00          53.1%         66.5%     98.0%        3.2%    0.142    10.2

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16      37.5%       25.0% 
   0.05-0.15      45      60.0%       47.7% 
   0.15-0.35      68      81.8%       29.4% 
       >0.35     367     100.0%       59.5% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

observation shows the network has a receptive field, which it has
before any training at all.
